## Review Triage Log — settled source refresh, 2026-10-18

All three configured context-free layers reviewed frozen **272,148-byte** input `3ee6bb2cc2f40617eef37ef1c1e49092ebce484255fa7bdd5c99600abe118e0b`: the current two-document preparation/owner-continuation delta plus externally authored Platform compiled/test, Builds catalog/workflow and EventStore source deltas since the prior preparation. All 10 blind, six edge, one verification-gap and one Other finding have separate dispositions below. No source/test file was edited by this continuation. The owner restriction preserves original in-progress frontmatter/baseline/review-loop and records follow-ups only in these two existing documents.

| Finding | Verdict | Route | Verified evidence / disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| RF-BH1 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-host | Actual Agents.UI uses Microsoft.NET.Sdk.Razor and has no executable entry point; AddProject treats it as a runnable host. The synthetic Web fixture does not establish real UI launch. Platform owner must supply the real receiving web host before any positive Agents-host claim. |
| RF-BH2 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-infrastructure | AgentsHostComposition adds projects without sidecars/state/invocation/domain-registration wiring, while AgentDomainHostComposition requires Dapr and leaves several adapters deferred. An accepted optional profile is insufficient for working Agents readiness. |
| RF-BH3 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-bindings | The host emits Agents__Parties__Binding and Agents__Conversations__Binding, while the actual adapters select Parties:BaseUrl/Tenant and Agents:EventStore:BaseUrl. Those bindings do not activate the supported live clients/dispatcher. |
| RF-BH4 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-admission | TryCompose supplies null capabilities; the nonnull overload registers private services in the AppHost only. AdmitAsync has no receiving-process production caller. This does not establish a functioning cross-process replay/spool admission boundary. |
| RF-BH5 | medium | defer-existing-Agents-bootstrap | Tenants BootstrapHostedService constructs BootstrapGlobalAdmin with the configured literal UserId. Passing a reference label through Tenants__BootstrapGlobalAdminUserId does not resolve it to the intended administrator. |
| RF-BH6 | medium | defer-existing-authority-contract | QueryCallerAuthorityBinding emits only its digest; WorkloadAuthorizationDetails.Decode requires tenant/domain for every nonempty binding set, and AcquireBoundAssertionAsync invokes it before transport. Asymmetric caller-query issuance therefore refuses that producer output. |
| RF-BH7 | maybe-false | defer-unverified-authority-policy | Exact static grants do require every requested digest value to be enrolled. Whether generic fresh-query signing is an intended supported authority capability is unresolved: the issuer explicitly promises exact-binding grants, and no approved dynamic-digest authorization policy is present. EventStore authority owner must settle that policy before claiming general fresh-query support; do not weaken the grant check. |
| RF-BH8 | medium | defer-existing-recovery-integrity | For an original pending Match(ETag) envelope without a terminal anchor, recovery validates prior-value presence but not an independent digest. CompensateAsync restores PreviousBytes; its later anchor hashes the already-read bytes, so a different valid previous JSON value can be restored after a conflict. Reopen with persisted-value corruption evidence before stronger recovery-integrity claims. |
| RF-BH9 | medium | defer-existing-query-page-mapping | DaprDomainQueryInvoker preserves only InvalidEnvelope/InvalidCursor for HTTP400. A supported InvalidPage result is converted to ActorException, losing client-error classification; share the same follow-up as RF-EH5. |
| RF-BH10 | medium | defer-existing-profile-reference-validation | OptionalReference checks size/whitespace and a credential-substring blacklist, without requiring a reference scheme. Compact raw credential-shaped strings pass and are forwarded to project environment. The optional Agents profile owner must define and validate supported reference schemes before asserting secret exclusion. |
| RF-EH1 | medium | defer-existing-authority-contract | Independently traced the same digest-only producer to mandatory tenant/domain decode and bound issuance refusal. This row retains the separate edge finding; action shares RF-BH6. |

| RF-EH6 | medium | defer-existing-delegated-response-metadata | DelegatedOwnerQueryClient calls the two-argument FromPayload with query.QueryType as projectionType; that overload discards SubmitQueryResponse.Metadata. PostgreSQL ceilings are 8 CPU, 16 GiB memory with the same swap ceiling, 100 connections, a 40-connection Dapr pool and 400 GiB tmpfs maximum. This maximum is neither a reservation nor a measured demand. Host capacity/headroom and total graph capacity remain unobserved and must be checked before startup in the later authorized operation. Storage is ephemeral run-owned tmpfs; no retained disk is formatted or resized. Private TLS keys, observer password file, local file secrets, gate and scheduler state stay within the existing private run workspace. Development UTC and Debug source builds are disclosed local limitations.

One initial request owns mapping/build/startup within 14,400 seconds, with the existing 600-second startup cap and separate 900-second stopping cap. Fresh readiness must be at most 60 seconds old and leave at least 4,501 seconds to execute. Startup includes the actual registered CRUD/ETag/transaction/actor-metadata/TTL/bounds/authority probes; offline doubles provide no runtime probe credit. One writer submits 125 records for A, waits at least 1,800 actual UTC and monotonic seconds, then submits 125 for B. Before any cohort, the exact fixed read-only SQL must execute successfully against the actual Dapr-created schema and return no A rows. After B, capture all actual A payload/TTL bounds at A+3,540 seconds, then strongly read A at A+3,590 seconds with at most sixteen concurrent existing GETs in fixed key order. Observe A expiry/absence and reminder/index progress by the existing +900-second grace, and verify all canonical B records unchanged throughout. A SQL/schema error refuses the operation before cohort submission; offline doubles do not satisfy this preflight. PostgreSQL observations use only the fixed read-only observer/TCP/verify-full queries. No simulated time or record backdating is allowed in that operation.

Each cohort has at most two submissions total, with the second permitted only for uncertain transport using the same identity and timestamp. No automatic infrastructure restart, new run, cleanup retry, cohort-clock reset or deadline extension is permitted. Drift, invalid/expired gate, withdrawal, incorrect observations, semantic failure or budget exhaustion stops native work and closes the gate. Success requires actual owned cleanup. A failed local run keeps the existing FR-4 retained-resource policy, with new local workload, purge and heartbeat mutations refused, the first failed outcome preserved, and actual retained/remaining/unknown disposition recorded. Already-admitted SDK operations may finish; subsequent mutation boundaries refuse closure. A failed one-shot capability attempt may leave its finite probe keys in that retained backend as diagnostic failure state; this earns no cleanup credit and cannot trigger more probe attempts. Withdrawal follows the runner's bounded automatic cleanup. Gate-closure failure or unverified quiescence/cleanup remains a failure; no pass or continuing workload authority is inferred.

Do not append an automatic `down` invocation to a retained failure: current `DownAsync(string, ...)` starts a fresh selected 900-second budget, so doing that would reset the approved stopping budget. Retained infrastructure disposal requires its own later concrete owner action against the actual returned run identity; it grants no workload restart. Retain the native TRX, detailed observation artifact/hash, exact source/runtime/backend/readiness identities, first terminal outcome and actual cleanup disposition through the existing result/evidence paths. No new report protocol or functional-to-full predecessor conversion is introduced.

## Intent

Deliver the smallest real PostgreSQL retention experiment through Hexalith.Platform's supported local run-owned environment. Platform owns infrastructure and lifecycle; Memories consumes its existing runner descriptor/result and verifies behavior. One existing story, verifier and consolidated report; no new framework, AppHost, runner, receipt protocol or numbered specification.

| Retry budget | At most **two VM launch attempts total**, each with a 10-minute readiness limit; a second only for a transport/readiness transient after verified stop. Same QEMU syntax/device/resource error stops the run. Read-only commands: at most three attempts, 2/5-second delays, 120-second aggregate command bound. Downloads: at most two attempts within the staging cap. Mutations: no blind retry; re-read UID/resourceVersion and reconcile the same intended object. Cohort emit: at most two requests with the same run/segment/time identity, only for transport uncertainty, and require exact accounting. No second cohort experiment or reset of elapsed time on semantic failure. |
| Observation/outputs | Poll at most every 30 seconds with per-query deadlines. At most 1 MiB per retained stdout/stderr stream and 256 MiB total run evidence, plus the separate 20-GiB staging cap. Exceeded bounds fail with a retained reason. |
| Stop and restoration | Stop tests on denial, drift, withdrawal, expiry, resource floor violation or failed semantic assertion. Separately preapprove up to 15 minutes of restoration after a stop: disable only the run-owned gate, release only its Lease, scale only its writer/lifecycle/clock to zero, retain evidence/storage, then stop only the UUID/PID/start-time/argv-matching guest. Never delete namespaces/PVCs/disks as cleanup. Unverifiable cleanup makes the run incomplete and blocks another launch. |

The corrected disk vector is `-drive file=...,if=none,id=qual-os,format=raw,...` plus `-device virtio-blk-pci,drive=qual-os,serial=memories-qual-os`, with the equivalent data pair. Serial is not a raw backend option. Preserve paths/UUID and exact installed tool bytes; do not run either failed vector. One independent host deadline must bound the guest even if the agent dies. API cleanup must be observed before shutdown; stopped QEMU alone is not gate/Lease/replica cleanup.

A single later operational approval covers the complete exact setup/test/restore plan. Material scope changes and exhausted budgets require new authority; ordinary in-budget retries do not. No repeat standing-policy approval. Full campaign continuation and its 72-hour/168-hour mismatch are later work, not this slice.

| BH9 | high | patch | OpenBao unseal key appears in Kubernetes exec arguments. Send the unseal request payload through protected stdin. |
| BH10 | medium | patch | Guest node wait retries all RuntimeError values for 300 seconds. Replace with an explicitly bounded readiness watch; do not retry semantic failures. |

| EC2 | high | patch | Retained disk identities are checked only before potentially long staging. Revalidate immediately before QEMU spawn. |
| EC3 | high | patch | Same demonstrated unchecked guest-consumption path as BH6; downloaded transport bytes need final consumption checks. |
| EC4 | medium | patch | Same startup read classification as BH4/BH5 causes both false 120-second refusal and mutation replay. |
| EC5 | high | patch | Gate disable and scaling verify objectUID but not the current Lease holder. Recheck ownership before every restoration mutation, including after release. |
| EC6 | high | patch | Preparation copies archives before aggregate capacity/free-space checks. Enforce the existing limits before and throughout writes. |
| EC7 | high | patch | Same demonstrated missing reference/build closure as BH2; Directory.Packages.props imports Hexalith.Builds input outside the current map. |
| VG1 | medium | patch | The filed searches establish no CI discovery of the new suite. Register it in the existing CI path with its local render prerequisites. |

| VG3 | high | patch | The filed searches establish no execution test of the formatting safeguard. Add sandboxed disk-identity/blankness/single-attempt tests without real devices. |
| VG4 | medium | patch | Authority tests use real datetime.now against a fixed 2026 window. Pin the fixture clock, preserving explicit expiry-negative cases. |

## Intent

**Problem:** Story 27.4 needs an owner-approved bounded run, complete PG2 C1 predecessor, live C2–C6 evidence and A41 publication proof.

**Approach:** Use the adopted sole-owner policy, prepare one bounded run record for owner approval, collect and verify C1 and qualification evidence, then record owner acceptance and verify A41 publication.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** Bind authority and evidence to approved `PG-ONPREM-2` SHA-256 `95aa36e8aba8d9914566f6df894c33098739251040dd0cc4b2917f76a994ccdb` and workload SHA-256 `71903bb8cc1889a015e066b0276fba2c7f073b2bdfc4d3b11225fc79ec6f091f`. Check current owner approval, expiry and withdrawal. Require 25 verified C1 gates in one eligible session, explicit fault/purge scopes, distinct owner-accepted C5/C6 records, and disabled gate, empty Lease and zero lifecycle/clock replicas at finish. Use private owner-controlled hashed/readback evidence for non-Production qualification; separate receipt services, independent human reviewers and external immutable custody are not required for this mode.

**Never:** Treat policy adoption, historical PG1 C1.15, closed-window C1.16, fixtures or role labels as a live run approval. Do not claim independent human review for the sole owner’s decisions. Contact an unapproved target, enable Production writes, weaken predecessor refusal, expose secrets, change the zero-replica overlay, or resolve A41 and its sprint action before publication proof.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Outcome | Failure Behavior |
|----------|---------------|------------------|------------------|
| Hold | Missing, expired, changed or withdrawn owner run approval or C1 | `RECHECK_ONLY`; no target or A41 mutation | Name the missing fact |
| Qualification | Current owner run approval, verified C1, scoped C2–C4 actions | Hash-bound packets prove acknowledgement, retention, faults, audit continuity and privacy denial | Reject partial proof and restore disabled/empty/zero |
| Close-out | Owner-accepted C0–C6, terminal proof and publication approval | Exact A41 transition, protected bytes intact, remote containment proven | Reject drift or missing approval |

</frozen-after-approval>

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given missing, withdrawn, expired or changed owner run approval, when readiness or a producer is invoked, then it refuses before target access and leaves Production writes and A41 unchanged.
- Given accepted same-session C1 and scoped qualification approval, when C0/C2–C4 run, then hash-bound packets prove required lifecycle behavior and final disabled/empty/zero state.
- Given accepted C0–C6 and terminal evidence, when close-out runs, then only registered A41 paths change, protected history stays byte-identical, and the published commit contains the approved transition.
